# 06 — Hostile document (injection barricade)

This file is a live attack corpus. Every section below contains real,
un-escaped control bytes, hostile URLs, or structural bombs, embedded in
ordinary markdown. It exists to prove stele's stated security property:
**document text can never emit a control sequence.**

`sanitize()` in `crates/stele/src/painter.rs` strips C0 (0x00-0x1F), DEL
(0x7F), C1 (0x80-0x9F) and the bidi override/isolate code points
(U+202A-202E, U+2066-2069) from every run before it reaches the wire.
`hyperlink::sanitize_url()` in `crates/highlight/src/hyperlink.rs`
allowlists `http`, `https`, `file`, `mailto` and nothing else, then strips
control bytes and `;` from what survives.

## HOW TO READ THIS FILE

Open it in stele and watch the terminal, not just the text. Everything
here must render **inert** — either as visible literal characters (with the
control byte silently removed) or as nothing at all. Nothing here may
*execute*.

**If the terminal visibly changes state while you view this file, that is a
BUG — report it, and name the section it happened in.** Specifically:

| Symptom | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| Window/tab title changes | OSC 0/1/2 escaped the barricade |
| Colors change, text turns red/green/inverse, background flips | SGR CSI escaped |
| Cursor jumps, hides, or the screen clears/scrolls oddly | Cursor/erase/scroll CSI escaped |
| The alt screen flips, or the view freezes | `?1049h` or `?2026h` escaped |
| Your clipboard contents change | OSC 52 escaped — a real exfiltration primitive |
| Images already on screen vanish | A forged kitty `a=d` APC escaped |
| Text after a section disappears or gets swallowed | An unterminated string sequence escaped |
| A visible hyperlink points somewhere other than what it reads | OSC 8 or bidi spoofing escaped |
| stele hangs, spikes RAM, or aborts | A structural or math bomb defeated a guard |

Also worth reporting: any raw control byte visible in a `stele > file`
capture. `crates/stele/tests/painter_frame.rs::assert_only_known_escapes`
is the machine-checked version of that same rule.

A note on what is *not* stripped, so expectations here stay honest:
zero-width space, soft hyphen, and Unicode Tags characters pass through
`sanitize()` untouched. They are not control code points and carry no
terminal state, so they are inert by nature rather than by removal.

---

## 1. Raw ESC in prose

*expect: the literal characters `[31mred`, `]0;t`, `_Gx` and `Pq` shown inline with the ESC gone; no color change, no cursor movement, no title change*

A bare escape byte here -><- sits between two arrows. This one starts a
CSI: [31mred text should not actually be red[0m. This one starts an
OSC: ]0;t and this one an APC: _Gx and this one a DCS: Pq — all
four introducers appear mid-sentence with no terminator in reach.

Other C0 bytes in prose: BEL-><- BS-><- DEL-><- vertical
tab-><- form feed-><- and a bare CR-><- that would otherwise
rewind this line over itself.

## 2. Heading with a raw ESC [1;31m and an OSC ]0;pwned

*expect: the heading above renders as literal text including `[1;31m` and `]0;pwned`; the window title is unchanged*

The heading line above is the payload. If the terminal title bar now says
`pwned`, or the heading is red rather than the theme's heading color, the
barricade failed at heading runs.

## 7. CSI sequences — color, cursor, erase, scroll region

*expect: every sequence below shows as literal text starting with `[`; the cursor never moves, the screen never clears, no scroll region is set, and colors stay as the theme painted them*

- SGR bold red on white: [1;31;47m payload [0m
- SGR 256-color: [38;5;196m payload [39m
- SGR truecolor: [38;2;255;0;0m payload [0m
- SGR reset-everything: [m and [0m
- Cursor home: [H
- Cursor absolute: [10;40H and [10;40f
- Cursor up/down/fwd/back: [5A [5B [5C [5D
- Cursor save/restore: 7 and 8 (and [s / [u)
- Erase in display: [0J [1J [2J [3J (3J drops scrollback)
- Erase in line: [0K [1K [2K
- Insert/delete lines: [5L [5M
- Scroll region set to rows 1-5: [1;5r then reset [r
- Scroll up/down: [5S [5T
- Device attributes query: [c and [>c and [0c
- Cursor position report query: [6n
- Full reset (RIS): c
- Soft reset (DECSTR): [!p
- Set columns to 132 (DECCOLM): [?3h

## 8. DECSET private modes

*expect: all of these render as literal text beginning with `[?`; in particular the alt screen must not flip, the display must not freeze, and the cursor must stay visible. `assert_only_known_escapes` deliberately refuses `?` in CSI parameters so a private mode can never masquerade as one of the painter's own sequences*

- Alt screen enter/leave: [?1049h ... [?1049l
- Legacy alt screen: [?47h / [?47l and [?1047h
- Synchronized update (the mode stele itself wraps frames in — a forged
  begin with no end would freeze the display): [?2026h ... [?2026l
- A begin with no matching end: [?2026h
- Hide cursor / show cursor: [?25l ... [?25h
- Bracketed paste on/off: [?2004h ... [?2004l
- Application cursor keys: [?1h / [?1l
- Autowrap off: [?7l
- Mouse tracking on: [?1000h [?1002h [?1003h [?1006h
- Focus reporting: [?1004h
- Origin mode: [?6h
- Reverse video (whole screen): [?5h
- Save/restore private modes: [?1049s and [?1049r

## 9. OSC sequences — title, hyperlink injection, clipboard, color queries

*expect: literal text only: `]0;...`, `]8;;...`, `]52;...`, `]10;?`. The window title must not change, the clipboard must not change, no reply must be typed back into the terminal, and no visible text may become a hyperlink to anywhere the document names here*

- Set icon name + title: ]0;PWNED-BY-06-HOSTILE
- Set icon name only: ]1;PWNED
- Set window title, ST-terminated: ]2;PWNED\
- OSC 8 hyperlink injection around innocent text: ]8;;https://evil.example/stealCLICK ME]8;;
- OSC 8 with a javascript target: ]8;;javascript:alert(1)\text]8;;\
- OSC 8 with extra `;` fields to break out of the URI: ]8;id=x;https://a.example;https://b.example\
- **OSC 52 clipboard write** (real exfiltration primitive) — if your
  clipboard now contains `pwned`, this is a critical bug: ]52;c;cHduZWQ=
- OSC 52 clipboard *read* request: ]52;c;?
- OSC 10 foreground color query: ]10;?
- OSC 11 background color query: ]11;?
- OSC 11 background color *set* to red: ]11;rgb:ffff/0000/0000
- OSC 12 cursor color set: ]12;#ff0000
- OSC 4 palette slot 1 set to red: ]4;1;#ff0000
- OSC 104 palette reset: ]104
- OSC 7 working-directory notify: ]7;file:///etc
- OSC 9 desktop notification: ]9;stele has been pwned
- OSC 777 notify: ]777;notify;title;body

## 10. APC — forged kitty graphics commands

*expect: literal text beginning with `_G`; **any image already on your screen must survive this section.** stele speaks the kitty graphics protocol itself, so a document-supplied APC is the highest-value forgery here*

- Delete all images (`a=d,d=A`): _Ga=d,d=A\
- Delete all images including data: _Ga=d,d=A,q=2\
- Delete by id: _Ga=d,d=i,i=1\
- Delete by placement: _Ga=d,d=p,i=1,p=1\
- Forged transmit-and-display with junk payload: _Ga=T,f=100,s=1,v=1;QUJDRA==\
- Forged transmit with a file path (`t=f`) pointing at a secret: _Ga=T,f=100,t=f;L2V0Yy9wYXNzd2Q=\
- Forged animation frame: _Ga=f,i=1,r=1\
- Forged query: _Ga=q,i=31\
- APC with a BEL terminator instead of ST: _Ga=d,d=A
- Bare 8-bit APC introducer: Ga=d,d=A

*expect: literal text beginning with `P`, `X`, `^`; the sequences must not put the terminal into a string-consuming state, and the paragraph after each one must still be visible*

- DCS sixel image: Pq#0;2;0;0;0#0~~@@vv@@~~@@~~$-\ — and this text too.

- DCS ReGIS: PpS(E)(P[100,100])(V[200,200])\ — and this.
- SOS (start of string): Xanything at all can go here\ — and this.
- PM (privacy message): ^hidden payload\ — and this.
- Bare 8-bit DCS/SOS/PM: q~~ x y — and this.
- A lone 7-bit ST with nothing opened: \ — and this.
- A lone 8-bit ST:  — and this.

## 13. Raw 8-bit C1 control bytes (U+0080-U+009F)

*expect: nothing from this section reaches the wire. `sanitize()` filters on code points 0x80-0x9F, and `assert_only_known_escapes` panics on any raw byte in 0x80-0x9F. Note these are C1 *code points*, UTF-8 encoded as C2 80..C2 9F in this file — a bare 0x9B byte would make the file invalid UTF-8 and the loader would reject the whole document before the painter ever saw it, which is a different (also correct) defense*

- CSI (U+009B) + SGR red: 31m payload 0m
- CSI + alt screen: ?1049h
- CSI + clear screen: 2J
- CSI + cursor home: H
- OSC (U+009D) + title: 0;pwned-8bit
- OSC + clipboard: 52;c;cHduZWQ=
- APC (U+009F) + kitty delete: Ga=d,d=A
- DCS (U+0090): 0;1|17/1b
- ST (U+009C) alone: 
- NEL (U+0085) mid-line: beforeafter
- IND (U+0084): ab   RI (U+008D): cd   HTS (U+0088): ef
- The whole C1 block in one run:  <- between the colons above and this arrow, nothing should be visible

## 14. Bidi overrides and isolates (Trojan Source)

*expect: the override and isolate characters are stripped, so text reads in logical order: `userexe.txt`, `admin`, `if (accessgranted)`. The directional *marks* LRM/RLM/ALM are NOT stripped — legitimate RTL text needs them — so the marked line may still shape as RTL*

Filename spoof (RLO ... PDF) — must not read as `filename.exe`:

- `user‮txt.exe‬` and `invoice‮fdp.exe‬`
- LRO/PDF: `‭forced-ltr‬`  RLE/PDF: `‫embedded-rtl‬`  LRE/PDF: `‪embedded-ltr‬`
- First strong isolate: `⁨isolated⁩`  LRI: `⁦lri⁩`  RLI: `⁧rli⁩`

Trojan-source style comment-out, inside a code block:

```rust
if access_level != "user‮ ⁦// Check if admin⁩ ⁦" {
    grant_admin();
}
```

Link-target spoof — the visible text must not be reorderable to disguise
where the link goes:

[safe-site.example‮/moc.live//:sptth‬](https://example.com/actually-here)

Directional marks that must SURVIVE (this is not a bug):

- LRM: `a‎b`  RLM: `a‏b`  ALM: `a؜b`
- Real Arabic with an RLM: `مرحبا‏ world`

## 15. Other invisible characters

*expect: no terminal state change and no crash. These are NOT stripped by `sanitize()` — they are not control code points — so they pass through as inert text. Expect them to render as nothing, as a placeholder box, or to affect line breaking. Column alignment may shift; terminal state must not*

- Zero-width space (U+200B): `a​b​c`
- Zero-width non-joiner (U+200C) / joiner (U+200D): `a‌b` `a‍b`
- Word joiner (U+2060): `a⁠b`
- Soft hyphen (U+00AD): `hy­phen­ation`
- Zero-width no-break space / BOM (U+FEFF): `a﻿b`
- Mongolian vowel separator (U+180E): `a᠎b`
- Hangul filler (U+3164) and Braille blank (U+2800): `aㅤb⠀c`
- Invisible operators (U+2062, U+2063): `a⁢b⁣c`
- Interlinear annotation anchors (U+FFF9-U+FFFB): `a￹b￺c￻d`
- Object replacement (U+FFFC) and replacement char (U+FFFD): `a￼b�c`
- Unicode Tags block — a full hidden ASCII message:
  `start󠀁󠁲󠁭󠀠󠀭󠁲󠁦󠀠󠀯󠁿end`
- Bare tag characters (U+E0041, U+E0060, U+E007E): `a󠁁b󠁠c󠁾d`
- Variation selectors (U+FE00, U+E0100): `a︀b󠄀c`
- Combining mark stack (Zalgo, 40 marks on one base): `é̂̃̄̅̆̇̈̉̊́̂̃̄̅̆̇̈̉̊́̂̃̄̅̆̇̈̉̊́̂̃̄̅̆̇̈̉̊`

## 18. Hostile image paths

*expect: no image is fetched over the network, no file outside the document's own directory is rendered, and no bytes of any file named here appear on screen. Each of these should fail to decode and fall back to its alt text (sanitized). Paths are resolved as `base_dir.join(dest)`, so an absolute path does reach the filesystem as a *read attempt* — the guarantee is that a non-image file can never render, not that the open is never attempted*

- Remote http: ![remote png](http://evil.example/track.png)
- Remote https: ![remote https](https://evil.example/track.png?exfil=1)
- Protocol-relative: ![proto rel](//evil.example/x.png)
- data: URI image: ![data uri](data:image/png;base64,iVBORw0KGgo=)
- file: URI image: ![file uri](file:///etc/passwd)
- Traversal: ![traversal](../../../../../../etc/passwd)
- Traversal to a key: ![ssh key](../../../../../../../.ssh/id_rsa)
- Absolute sensitive path: ![passwd](/etc/passwd)
- Absolute shadow: ![shadow](/etc/shadow)
- Absolute device: ![random](/dev/urandom)
- Absolute fifo-ish: ![zero](/dev/zero)
- macOS keychain: ![keychain](/Library/Keychains/System.keychain)
- Home-relative: ![aws creds](~/.aws/credentials)
- Path with a raw newline in it: ![newline path](img/small.png
/etc/passwd)

*expect: a single logical paragraph wrapped across many screen rows, scrollable at interactive speed. Must not hang. The highlighter's pathological-line guard (`THREADED_GUARD_THRESHOLD`) is what this exercises*
